# Appendix A: weight-conditioning experiments on full fine-tunes (CLIP ViT-B/32 and ViT-B/16, 8 tasks)

Generated by `make_tables.py` from the raw result files of `/data/home/azpitarte/condmerge` (git HEAD at generation: b4267f7 (refs/heads/master)). Every number below was computed by the script from those files (list and MD5s in F1). Hand-written content is limited to captions (which state design parameters such as grid sizes, split fractions and counts transcribed from the code and configs; several are asserted against the data inside the script) and to the quotations of documentation claims in X0/X1, which are the objects being checked.

**Conventions.** Accuracy metrics are in percent with 2 decimals; differences are in percentage points (pp). `mean_norm` = normalised accuracy (each task's accuracy divided by that task's own-expert accuracy on the same split, averaged over the tasks); `mean_acc` = mean absolute accuracy; `min_norm` = worst task's normalised accuracy. dev = 20 % of each official test set (selection only), test = the other 80 % (frozen configurations only). Row labels (rule names, tags, parameter names) are copied from the files; the pipe in a tag such as `uniform|tuned` is escaped as `\|` inside Markdown tables. The reference for all test differences is `uniform|tuned` (B/32) and `b16xfer|uniform|tuned` (B/16).

*Note:* Notation in the expressions: full(s) = statistic s of the full expert weight W_i per map (kappa2_raw = s1/sq from a float64 SVD of the stored float32 matrix; kappa2 = same but +inf when the numerical rank is below q; smax/smin = extreme singular values; kappa_q90 = s1 / s_{ceil(0.9 q)}; kappa_plus_0.001 = s1 / s_r on the r singular values above 1e-3 s1); upd(s) = statistic of the task update D_i = W_i - W0; the *_sup kinds are spectra of M C^{1/2} (C = pooled calibration second moment, budget B); zscore = within-map standardisation across experts; `_rev` = negated score (larger statistic -> larger weight).

*Note:* Layerwise rules apply the softmax independently to each of the 73 maps; `global:<rule>` replaces the per-map scores by one parameter-weighted mean per expert; temperature beta = b / scale (scale = median over maps of the across-expert s.d. of the scores) except the absolute-beta rules gd_invvar, global:gd_invvar.

### L2. Legend of tags, variants and metrics

**Varied / split / metric / units:** Meaning of the tag suffixes, the metric names and units used in all tables of this appendix.

**Source:** `docs/PROTOCOL.md`; `experiments/make_freeze_list.py`; `experiments/freeze_eval.py`; `configs/b16_transfer_configs.json`  
**Rows:** 13

| term | meaning |
|:---|:---|
| RULE\|tuned | frozen configuration = dev-best (b, alpha) over the rule's whole grid (experiments/make_freeze_list.py) |
| RULE\|alpha1 | frozen configuration = dev-best b at alpha = 1 (the literal normalised average) |
| best_single | single:EXPERT, alpha = 1: the expert with the highest dev mean_norm in the 8x8 cross matrix (cars), evaluated alone |
| b16xfer\|RULE\|tuned (or alpha1) | the B/32-frozen config of RULE evaluated once on ViT-B/16 (configs/b16_transfer_configs.json) |
| b16xfer\|NULL@BASE\|tuned | shuffled-score null NULL (e.g. shuf0:kappa) evaluated at exactly the frozen (b, alpha) of BASE\|tuned |
| w1 / w2 / w3 | frozen_configs_w1/w2/w3.json: freeze waves first committed in edbdcf5 / ee218d2 / 8af9dca |
| mean_norm | mean over the 8 tasks (or the subset's tasks) of accuracy divided by that task's own-expert accuracy on the same split (%) |
| mean_acc | mean over tasks of absolute top-1 accuracy (%) |
| min_norm | smallest per-task normalised accuracy (%) |
| n_eff | exp(entropy of the per-map coefficient vector), averaged over the 73 maps (8 = uniform, 1 = one expert per map) |
| dev / test | dev = 20 % of each official test split (permutation seed 0), used for all selection; test = the other 80 %, only touched by frozen configs (docs/PROTOCOL.md) |
| b, alpha, beta | beta = b / scale(rule); b = inf = per-map argmin; W = W0 + alpha sum_i lambda_i D_i; alpha = 1 is the normalised average; uniform with alpha = K s is Task Arithmetic with scale s |
| pp | percentage points (difference of two percentages) |

### A0. Headline comparison: key rules on dev, frozen ViT-B/32 test and ViT-B/16 transfer

**Varied / split / metric / units:** For the key rules, dev mean_norm at alpha = 1 and tuned (best over the shared grid, with its (b, alpha)), the frozen ViT-B/32 TEST mean_norm / mean_acc and difference to uniform|tuned with its 95 % CI, the alpha = 1 test mean_norm, and the ViT-B/16 transfer test mean_norm and difference to uniform|tuned; mean_norm and mean_acc in %, differences in pp; selection on dev (20 %), test on the other 80 %.

*Note:* kappa = kappa2_raw = s_1/s_q from a float64 SVD of the stored float32 matrix, q = min(m, n); layer -1 = patch embedding. 'rank' is rank_default = #{s_i > max(m,n) * eps32 * s_1}; 'fp32-singular' means rank_default < q, which is exactly where kappa2 (kappa_strict) is +inf. rel_spread and max_rel_shift are defined in experiments/analyze_spectra.py (population s.d., ddof = 0). 'fp32 noise sd / across-expert sd' = s.d. over experts of (kappa from an fp32 SVD minus kappa from an fp64 SVD) divided by the s.d. over experts of kappa (the 'fp32 rounding share'; a value near or above 1 means the rounding error of kappa is as large as its spread across experts).

### A2b. Per linear map: task-update norms, stable rank and first-order probe, CLIP ViT-B/32 full fine-tunes

**Varied / split / metric / units:** One row per linear map: the base Frobenius norm, the Frobenius norm of the task updates D_i = W_i - W0 (median over the 8 experts, [min, max]), the update stable rank, and how well the first-order rank-2 probe and the update norm relate to kappa across experts (weights only, no accuracy; norms are in weight units, ranks and correlations are dimensionless).

Correlations are Spearman across the 8 experts within the map (experiments/analyze_spectra.py); 'first-order' = kappa0 (u1' D v1/s1 - uq' D vq/sq), the linear rank-2 probe of MATH_NOTES N2, compared with the actual kappa shift across the 8 experts (Pearson r).

### A2c. Per linear map: condition numbers, rank flags and fp32 rounding share, CLIP ViT-B/16 full fine-tunes

**Varied / split / metric / units:** One row per linear map (the 73 matrices the rule acts on): the base model's kappa2 (kappa0), the min/median/max of kappa2(W_i) over the 8 experts, their relative spread, numerical-rank flags and the fp32-rounding share, from the SVDs of the stored weights (no accuracy involved; condition numbers are dimensionless).

*Note:* kappa = kappa2_raw = s_1/s_q from a float64 SVD of the stored float32 matrix, q = min(m, n); layer -1 = patch embedding. 'rank' is rank_default = #{s_i > max(m,n) * eps32 * s_1}; 'fp32-singular' means rank_default < q, which is exactly where kappa2 (kappa_strict) is +inf. rel_spread and max_rel_shift are defined in experiments/analyze_spectra.py (population s.d., ddof = 0). 'fp32 noise sd / across-expert sd' = s.d. over experts of (kappa from an fp32 SVD minus kappa from an fp64 SVD) divided by the s.d. over experts of kappa (the 'fp32 rounding share'; a value near or above 1 means the rounding error of kappa is as large as its spread across experts).

Ties are resolved as experiments/analyze_sweep.py does (first maximum in file order); the tie count shows whether another config has an identical mean_norm. * n_eff not recorded for uniform/metagpt: derived as exp(H(lambda_bar)) from the recorded global lambda (uniform = 8 by definition). 'diff vs uniform best' = this rule's best minus the best of uniform over its alpha grid (the Task-Arithmetic baseline).

*Note:* Full per-config data are in the shard files; per-rule full grids are summarised here only (the kappa and uniform grids are in A3d).

### A4a. Frozen ViT-B/32 test: all 101 rows of test_comparison.json, sorted by difference to the reference `uniform|tuned`

**Varied / split / metric / units:** Every dev-frozen configuration ('tuned' = best (b, alpha) over the shared grid, 'alpha1' = best b at alpha = 1, plus the best single expert) evaluated once on the TEST split (80 % of each test set) of the 8 ViT-B/32 experts; metrics = mean_norm / mean_acc / min_norm in % and the difference in mean_norm to the reference `uniform|tuned` in percentage points with a 95 % paired task-stratified bootstrap CI (2000 resamples of test images).

**Source:** `results/freeze/test_comparison.json`; `results/freeze/test.jsonl`; `results/freeze/frozen_configs_w1.json`; `results/freeze/frozen_configs_w2.json`; `results/freeze/frozen_configs_w3.json`

| transfer_comparison.json metrics = transfer_test.jsonl records | 27/27 tags | all |

### X0. Discrepancies between the docs and the raw results (13 substantive, 11 rounding-level or approximate, of 147 checks)

**Varied / split / metric / units:** Claims quoted from docs/REPORT_01_literal_rule.md, docs/FULL_REPORT.md, docs/RESEARCH_LOG.md and docs/SUMMARY.md whose value recomputed from the raw files by this script does not match exactly: substantive discrepancies first, then rounding-level (<= 1 unit in the last quoted digit) and approximate-claim entries; units as in the claim (mostly % and pp).

**Source:** `docs/REPORT_01_literal_rule.md`; `docs/FULL_REPORT.md`; `docs/RESEARCH_LOG.md`; `docs/SUMMARY.md`  
**Rows:** 24

| # | doc / section | claim | claimed | recomputed | status | note |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| 1 | REPORT_01 §2 / FULL §5 | kappa at alpha = 1, argmax b (report says b = 2) | 2 | 4 | DISCREPANCY | value at b=2 is 74.67, at b=4 74.80 (frozen kappa\|alpha1 uses b=4) |
| 2 | REPORT_01 §2 / FULL §5 | kappa_z at b = 4 (alpha = 1) mean_norm (%) | 66.1 | 66.5 | DISCREPANCY | b=8 gives 66.1; the quoted 66.1 matches b=8, not b=4 |
